### [1] [A Mathematical Model of Motivated Emotional Mind - Cognitive Embodied System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20437)
*Wiesław L. Galus,Janusz A. Starzyk*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文为具身智能系统提出"动机情绪心智"认知架构的数学模型，核心是对前馈、侧向交互与反馈构成的重入回路及表征选择机制进行严格形式化，并以"动机学习"统一驱动系统的稳态维持与行为选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习模型难以充分刻画受需求、情绪与调节状态塑造的具身系统动态，因此需要一个能整合需求阈值、目标生成与转移、身体状态、资源约束和动作不确定性的形式化框架。

Method: 构建数学模型：将外感受与内感受信号、身体—动机情境、记忆痕迹绑定为称为"semblions"的联想记忆结构；形式化重入回路（前馈处理、侧向交互、反馈通路）与表征竞争机制；涵盖次级知觉、好奇心、程序性缺口及旨在抑制稳态失衡（allostatic violation）的动作选择；以全局情绪作为中央控制信号调制学习率、表征效价与探索—利用平衡。

Result: 给出动机学习的严格形式化描述，明确semblions的绑定、竞争与自上而下重构过程，以及需求阈值、目标生成与目标转移、资源约束、动作不确定性等因素在反应选择中的作用机制。

Conclusion: 该模型是认知现象形式化的一步，为理论分析、计算机仿真及受生物过程启发的AI系统实现提供了基础。

Abstract: This article presents a mathematical model of the Motivated Emotional Mind cognitive architecture developed for embodied intelligent systems. Such a system learns to maintain its homeostasis through a generalized form of reinforcement learning based on its internal motivations, termed motivated learning (ML). The principal contribution of this article is a rigorous formalization of the re-entrant loop integrating feedforward processing, lateral interactions, and feedback pathways, together with the representational selection mechanisms that govern adaptive system responses. The model specifies how ongoing exteroceptive and interoceptive signals, bodily-motivational context, and memory traces are bound into associative memory structures termed semblions, which compete for access to further processing and top-down reconstruction. The formalization encompasses secondary perception, representational competition, curiosity, procedural gaps, and action selection directed toward limiting allostatic violations. Within this framework, motivated learning is tailored to embodied systems whose dynamics are shaped by needs, affect, and the current regulatory state. Unlike standard reinforcement-learning models, the proposed approach incorporates need thresholds, goal generation and shifting goals, bodily state, resource constraints, and action uncertainty, thereby providing a more adequate account of response selection under regulatory pressure. Global affect functions as a central control signal, modulating the learning rate, representational valence, and the balance between exploration and exploitation. The model presented here is a step toward a more rigorous formalization of cognitive phenomena and may provide a basis for further theoretical analysis, computer simulation, and implementation in artificial-intelligence systems inspired by biological processes.

</details>

### [2] [Neural Architectures of Slow and Fast Dynamics in the Human Brain](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2025.12.31.696728v3)
*Lu, Y.,Li, Z.,Mao, H.,Lyu, Q.,Lu, Y.,Yao, C.,Chen, J.,Tao, L.,Xiao, Z.,Tian, X.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究利用SPLIT方法分析大规模颅内sEEG数据，发现脑内存在可分离的快（~10-100 Hz）与慢（~1-10 Hz）时间尺度成分，构成双分时间架构，挑战了单一皮层时间尺度梯度的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脑在从快速感知与动作到缓慢生理调节的广阔时间范围上运作，以往认为这归因于单一的内在时间尺度皮层梯度。然而，一维模型无法解释局部回路如何同时支持快速外部行为和缓慢的身体耦合内部动态。

Method: 采用SPLIT（spectral piecewise-linear inference of timescales）方法，对大规模颅内立体脑电（sEEG）数据进行分析，共涉及8,619个电极触点、185名个体，分离出快、慢时间尺度成分。

Result: 仅快成分时间尺度遵循经典的从感觉运动到联合皮层的层级结构；慢成分时间尺度无层级梯度，而是与心率共变，并在具有心脏相关神经反应的位点富集。该双时间架构在清醒、静息、睡眠和麻醉状态下均持续存在。

Conclusion: 研究结果挑战了脑时间尺度的单一观点，揭示了一种内在的双分组织，其分别与皮层层级和神经生理（身体耦合）相关联。

Abstract: The human brain operates across a vast temporal range, from fast perception and action to slow physiological regulation. The capacity has attributed to a unitary cortical gradient of intrinsic timescales, yet such a unidimensional model cannot explain how local circuits simulateously support both rapid external behavior and slow internal body-coupled dynamics. Using SPLIT (spectral piecewise-linear inference of timescales) on a large-scale intracranial stereo-electroencephalography (8,619 contacts, 185 individuals), we identified dissociable fast (~10-100 Hz) and slow (~1-10 Hz) temporal components. Only the fast-component timescales followed the canonical sensorimotor-to-association cortical hierarchy. Slow-component timescales showed no hierarchical gradient, but were instead covaried with heart rate and enriched at site with heart-related neural responses. This dual temporal architecture persisted across wakefulness, rest, sleep, and anesthesia, challenging the unitary view of brain timescales and revealing an intrinsic bipartite organization that is associated with cortical hierarchy or neurophysiology.

</details>


### [3] [Online synaptic credit assignment in active dendrites](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.14.751357v1)
*He, G.,Zhang, S.,Du, K.,Huang, T.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究证明，详细多房室神经元模型中，电压对突触权重的梯度本身就是一个电压，可通过回放梯度电流精确复现，从而实现在线、因果的监督学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的信用分配通常被表述为抽象误差梯度的计算，但该梯度能否在生物物理细节丰富的多房室神经元模型中以物理、因果的形式存在，并支持在线监督学习，尚不清楚。

Method: 对标准模拟器求解的离散后向欧拉更新式进行微分，得到与原始电压动力学同形式的方程，由梯度电流驱动；将权重特定的梯度电流沿时间前向回放，并用回放的梯度电压与局部学习信号配对，构造因果在线学习规则。

Result: 在L5锥体神经元中，梯度回放在多种输入条件下均能高保真复现精确梯度（R2 > 0.998）；含活跃树突的单个L5锥体神经元可学会复现含钙平台和爆发活动的目标电压轨迹；详细神经元组成的循环网络可学会生成目标时间模式，显著优于仅训练读出层的对照。

Conclusion: 突触信用分配可由详细神经元的电压动力学在线实现，提示大脑中可能存在支持基于梯度的监督学习的物理基础。

Abstract: Credit assignment in neural networks is usually formulated as the computation of an abstract error gradient. Whether such a gradient can take a physical, causal form in biophysically detailed multi-compartment neuron models, and enable online, supervised learning, remains unclear. Here we show that the gradient of a detailed neuron's voltage with respect to a synaptic weight is itself a voltage. Differentiating the discrete backward-Euler update solved by standard simulators yields equations of the same form as the original voltage dynamics, driven by gradient currents. Replaying weight-specific gradient currents forward in time reproduces the exact gradient with high fidelity in an L5 pyramidal neuron across diverse input regimes (R2 > 0.998). Pairing the replayed gradient voltage with a local learning signal yields a causal, online learning rule. A single L5 pyramidal neuron with active dendrites learns to reproduce target voltage trajectories containing calcium plateaus and bursts, and recurrent networks of detailed neurons learn to generate target temporal patterns, far outperforming a readout-only control. These results demonstrate that synaptic credit assignment can be implemented online by the voltage dynamics of detailed neurons, potentially suggesting a physical substrate for gradient-based supervised learning in the brain.

</details>


### [4] [Adolescent development accelerates responses to input in human neocortical neurons](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.16.746058v1)
*Owen, S. F.,Kendrick, R. M.,Clark, J. K.,Leonard, E. P.,Ramasamy, N.,DeLope, S. S.,Phillips, H. W.,Buch, V. P.,Mahaney, K. B.,Grant, G. A.,Prolo, L. M.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究结合膜片钳电生理与单细胞测序（Patch-Seq），在儿童人脑切片与同龄小鼠脑切片中揭示人类L2/3锥体神经元出生后成熟的独特轨迹，并鉴定BK型钙激活钾通道为驱动放电动态变化的关键基因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哺乳动物新皮层在童年至青春期经历神经元生理特性与大规模网络的双重变化；这些生理变化在小鼠模型中已被充分理解，但人类新皮层中的情况知之甚少，亟需在人类组织层面直接解析其发育轨迹及分子机制。

Method: 将膜片钳电生理与单细胞测序（Patch-Seq）联用，作用于神经外科切除的儿童人脑切片及年龄匹配的小鼠脑切片；对Patch-Seq数据构建线性模型，识别可预测单细胞生理变异的基因；进一步通过药理学实验与计算建模验证候选通路。

Result: 人类L2/3锥体神经元表现出独特的出生后生理变化，与人类新皮层相较小鼠更具前馈、定向性网络结构相一致；人类特异性的放电序列动态变化包括更快的峰电位潜伏期以及早期放电的选择性加速。线性建模无偏地鉴定出BK型钙激活钾通道是童年至青春期人类放电序列动态变化的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了人类新皮层神经元出生后成熟的新机制，并展示了Patch-Seq在单细胞分辨率下解析基因—生理关系的全新应用价值。

Abstract: Through childhood and adolescence, profound changes to the physiology of individual neurons accompany large-scale network changes in the mammalian neocortex. These physiological changes are well understood in rodent models but far less is known in the human neocortex. Here we combine patch-clamp electrophysiology and single-cell sequencing (Patch-Seq) in neurosurgically-resected pediatric human brain slices and age-matched mouse brain slices to elucidate the unique developmental trajectory of human neurons. We find that human Layer 2/3 pyramidal neurons show distinctive postnatal changes in neuronal physiology that align with the more directed, feedforward network architecture of human neocortex relative to the mouse. Human-specific changes to spike train dynamics include faster spike latencies and selective acceleration of early spiking. By applying linear modeling to our Patch-Seq data, we identify genes that predict physiological variation across single cells. This unbiased approach unexpectedly identifies BK-type calcium-activated potassium channels as key drivers of human postnatal changes in spike train dynamics between childhood and adolescence. We further test this pathway through pharmacology and computational modeling. Together, our results reveal novel mechanisms of postnatal maturation in human neocortical neurons and demonstrate a new application of Patch-Seq to uncover gene-physiology relationships at single-cell resolution.

</details>


### [5] [Median raphe input to dorsal CA1 shapes VIP interneuron recruitment and novelty-guided spatial memory](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.17.752379v1)
*Luo, X.,Radhakrishnan, R.,Guet-McCreight, A.,Topolnik, D.,Bergeron, A.,Dulam, V.,Coulombe, L. D.,Besner, M.,Fortin-Houde, J.,Davies, J.,Michaud, F.,Amilhon, B.,Topolnik, L.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 中缝中核（MnR）投射通过谷氨酸能与血清素能协同机制调控背侧CA1的VIP中间神经元，进而组织海马去抑制，支持新异体验引导的探索与记忆编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表达血管活性肠肽的中间神经元（VIP-INs）在新异体验中门控海马抑制，但启动这些细胞的远距离信号来源尚不清楚。

Method: 结合解剖学映射与光遗传学记录、转录组与药理学分析、在体钙成像，并构建海马环路模型，配合行为学测试。

Result: MnR轴突支配多种VIP-IN亚型，通过快速谷氨酸能兴奋与血清素受体依赖的突触及内在反应性调节发挥作用；新异刺激优先募集速度耦合的VIP-IN集群，抑制MnR输入可选择性降低该反应幅度；环路模型将该通路与树突去抑制及位置细胞募集相联系；行为上抑制MnR输入保留探索参与但破坏空间采样组织并损害物体位置记忆。

Conclusion: MnR输入通过组织海马去抑制，支持新异引导的探索与记忆编码。

Abstract: Vasoactive intestinal peptide-expressing interneurons (VIP-INs) gate hippocampal inhibition during novel experience, but the long-range signals that engage these cells remain poorly understood. Here we identify median raphe (MnR) projections as a brainstem pathway that tunes dorsal CA1 VIP-IN recruitment through coordinated glutamatergic and serotonergic mechanisms. Anatomical mapping and optogenetic recordings showed that MnR axons innervate multiple VIP-IN subtypes, while transcriptomic and pharmacological analyses revealed fast glutamatergic excitation together with serotonin receptor-dependent modulation of synaptic and intrinsic responsiveness. In vivo calcium imaging showed that novelty preferentially recruited a speed-coupled VIP-IN ensemble, and inhibition of MnR input selectively reduced the magnitude of this response. A hippocampal circuit model linked this pathway to dendritic disinhibition and place-cell recruitment. Behaviorally, inhibition of MnR input preserved exploratory engagement but disrupted the organization of spatial sampling and impaired object-location memory. Thus, MnR input organizes hippocampal disinhibition to support novelty-guided exploration and memory encoding.

</details>


### [6] [Sleep spindles stabilize human thalamocortical networks, inhibiting pathological disruptions](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.13.751011v1)
*Jaber, K.,Ho, A.,Ye, H.,Bosque-Varela, P.,Aiello, G.,Avigdor, T.,Shenoy, K.,Ramani, P.,Wadi, L.,Thomas, J.,Klimes, P.,Richardson, R. M.,Southwell, D. G.,Hadar, P. N.,Cash, S. S.,Salami, P.,Carlson, D.,Zelmann, R.,Frauscher, B.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 利用癫痫患者颅内多夜记录，揭示睡眠纺锤波与发作间期癫痫样放电（IED）之间存在双向相互作用，纺锤波可作为丘脑皮层网络对病理活动的主动调节因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 丘脑通过纺锤振荡协调睡眠依赖的脑功能（记忆巩固、感觉门控、网络稳定性），且丘脑皮层回路在多种神经/精神疾病中受累，但人类丘脑皮层回路如何响应病理扰动尚不清楚。

Method: 以发作间期癫痫样放电（IED）作为自发扰动，对55名癫痫患者进行跨多个丘脑核团与皮层区域的多夜颅内记录，在毫秒到天的时间尺度上分析睡眠纺锤波与癫痫活动的相互作用。

Result: 睡眠纺锤波伴随IED抑制，而IED又提高随后的纺锤波出现概率，二者呈双向作用；IED较少的夜晚对应纺锤波发生增多、时程延长、频率加快，反映更稳定的丘脑皮层状态。

Conclusion: 睡眠纺锤波可能是丘脑皮层网络的潜在调节者，能主动响应并调节病理活动。

Abstract: The thalamus coordinates sleep-dependent brain function including memory consolidation, sensory gating, and network stability through spindle oscillations. Thalamocortical circuits are also affected in many neurological and neuropsychiatric disorders. Yet how human thalamocortical circuits respond to pathological disruptions remain poorly understood. Here, we leveraged interictal epileptiform discharges (IEDs) as spontaneous perturbations to probe spindle-generating circuits and thalamocortical dynamics in vivo. Using multi-night intracranial recordings spanning multiple thalamic nuclei and cortical regions in 55 individuals with epilepsy, we investigated interactions between sleep spindles and epileptic activity across timescales from milliseconds to days. Sleep spindles were associated with IED suppression, whereas IEDs increased subsequent spindle probability, revealing bidirectional interactions between physiological and pathological activity. Nights with lower IEDs corresponded to increased spindle occurrence, longer duration, and faster frequency, reflecting a more stable thalamocortical state. These findings propose sleep spindles as potential regulators of thalamocortical networks that actively respond to and regulate pathological activity.

</details>

### [7] [The Motile-Units model: Interacting spins model of cell polarization and motility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20587)
*Jonathan E. Ron,Nir S. Gov*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种用于二维细胞运动的粗粒化相互作用自旋模型，将细胞周长离散为随机二元自旋，活性自旋作为产力的"运动单元"，通过极性线索长程耦合，实现自发性对称破缺与自驱，并复现趋化、光遗传转向及竞争刺激下的方向决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究细胞迁移并拓展活性物质物理的适用范围，需要一个简洁框架，使细胞的速度与极性从内部随机自旋动力学中自发涌现，而非作为粒子层面的预设变量；同时希望便于纳入外部线索。

Method: 构建二维细胞运动的粗粒化相互作用自旋模型：将细胞周长离散为在活性与非活性态间切换的随机二元自旋，每个周长自旋代表"运动单元"，活性时产生突出力与逆行流；运动单元间通过由集体肌动蛋白逆行流平流输送的极性线索产生长程相互作用；另加入最近邻相互作用以调节速度与持续性。

Result: 模型呈现三个动力学相：随机行走相、持续随机行走相和以"跑—停"迁移为特征的间歇双稳态相；最近邻相互作用可调节速度与持续性但不改变整体相结构；框架能自然地纳入外部线索，复现趋化迁移、局域光遗传激活导向以及竞争刺激下的方向决策（对称破缺）。

Conclusion: 该模型引入一类新的活性粒子模型，其速度与极性均由内部随机自旋动力学涌现，而非作为粒子级变量强加，为细胞迁移研究提供框架，并拓展了活性物质物理的范围。

Abstract: We introduce a coarse-grained interacting-spin model for two-dimensional cell motility, in which the cell perimeter is discretized into stochastic binary spins that switch between active and inactive states. Each perimeter spin represents a "motile-unit" that is a source of protrusive force and retrograde flow when active. Long-range interactions between the motile-units arise through a polarity cue advected by the collective actin retrograde flow, providing a minimal realization of spontaneous symmetry breaking and self-propulsion. The model exhibits three dynamical phases, a random walk phase, persistent random walk phase, and an intermittent bistable phase characterized by run-and-tumble migration. Additional nearest-neighbor interactions modulate speed and persistence without altering the overall phase structure. Owing to its simplicity, the framework naturally incorporates external cues, reproducing chemotactic migration, steering by localized optogenetic activation, and directional decision-making (symmetry breaking) under competing stimuli. The model introduces a new class of active-particle model in which both speed and polarity emerge from internal stochastic spin dynamics, rather than being imposed as particle-level variables, offering a framework for the study of cell migration and extends the scope of active-matter physics.

</details>
