For each task, we construct high-fidelity reference posteriors using computationally heavy but established procedures such as rejection sampling and Markov chain Monte Carlo, enabling direct assessment of whether solvers recover the full set of solutions rather than the single best sample. We pair these references with a five-metric posterior evaluation suite: posterior-mean error, posterior-standard-deviation error, maximum mean discrepancy, sliced Wasserstein distance, and radially averaged power-spectrum error. These metrics assess pointwise accuracy, marginal uncertainty, distributional alignment, and global frequency fidelity. The benchmark spans sparse sensing, low-resolution observations, nonlinear forward models, varying noise levels, and multimodal priors, with a unified pipeline for distribution matching and uncertainty quantification. Our experiments reveal substantial distribution-matching gaps across current solvers, while showing that neural operators improve resolution robustness, and guidance weights and generation noise are key to posterior-variance calibration.

</details>


### [114] [Score Centering Stabilizes Off-policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20807)
*Martin Marek,Max Ryabinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型强化学习中训练与推理引擎不一致（TIM）导致的训练不稳定问题，发现其主因是随训练步累积的"漂移"偏置，并提出可加的"score centering"修正项来消除漂移、稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练大语言模型对训练引擎与推理引擎之间的微小差异高度敏感（即训练-推理不匹配，TIM）。但彻底消除TIM不现实，因为会严重牺牲rollout效率。因此作者试图在保留高效rollout的前提下寻找稳定RL的方法，探究TIM下不稳定的根本原因。

Method: 论文将TIM下的不稳定性归因于"漂移"——训练与推理引擎之间存在持续偏置，并随每个训练步积累。基于此推导出一个可加的"score centering"修正项，通过抵消漂移来稳定RL。由于该修正是可加的，它还能与重要性采样（importance sampling）方法组合使用。

Result: 在0.6B到30B参数规模的模型训练中，score centering单独使用即可匹配或超过量化条件下基于重要性采样的方法，且当不匹配越严重时优势越明显。在陈旧性（staleness）实验中，score centering与重要性采样的组合方案优于纯重要性采样基线。

Conclusion: TIM下RL不稳定的核心是累积漂移而非单纯分布差异；通过可加的score centering修正可有效抵消漂移、稳定训练。该方法不牺牲rollout效率，性能优于或媲美重要性采样，并可与其组合进一步提升效果，为大规模LLM的RL训练提供了一种高效稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) of large language models is notoriously sensitive to small differences between training and inference engines, often referred to as the training-inference mismatch (TIM). However, completely eliminating TIM is impractical, as it would come at a major cost to rollout efficiency. In this paper, we show that the instability of RL under TIM is primarily caused by drift: a persistent bias between training and inference engines that accumulates with every training step. We derive an additive "score centering" correction term that stabilizes RL under TIM by canceling drift. When training models from 0.6B to 30B parameters, score centering alone matches or outperforms methods based on importance sampling under quantization, with the gap growing as the mismatch becomes more severe. Because the correction is additive, score centering also composes with importance sampling -- their composition outperforms pure importance-sampling baselines in our staleness experiments.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Position Paper: Neurotransmitters as a Missing Dimension in Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20083)
*Yupei Li,Manuel Milling,Berrak Sisman,Björn Schuller*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文为立场论文，主张将神经调质（神经递质）机制作为继神经活动、突触可塑性之后的"第三条学习轴"显式建模进人工神经网络，以提升自适应与持续学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人工神经网络依赖统一、局部、基于梯度的参数更新，缺乏生物系统所具有的自适应灵活性与长期稳定性；主流应对方案多集中于架构扩展或正则化、参数隔离等数学微调手段，而忽视了神经递质信号与神经可塑性等内部学习原理，因此神经调质这一方向被严重低估。

Method: 立场性/观点性论文（positional paper），不提出具体算法或实验，而是类比哺乳动物大脑的适应性与可塑性，将神经调质界定为与神经活动、突触可塑性并列的第三条学习轴，并论证其应被显式纳入人工神经网络设计。

Result: 无实验或定量结果；核心产出为一项研究主张：在人工神经网络设计中引入神经调质原理，是一条有前景且尚未被充分探索的研究方向。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁研究界在自适应与持续学习系统的研发中给予神经调质视角更多关注，推动其成为深度学习的第三类学习机制。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks (ANNs), as core components of modern deep learning (DL) systems, lack the adaptive flexibility and long-term stability exhibited by biological systems. This limitation largely stems from the fact that conventional ANNs rely on uniform, local, and gradient-based parameter updates, while neglecting internal learning principles that are biological mechanisms such as neurotransmitters signalling or neuroplasticity. Consequently, many existing approaches focus on architectural expansion or mathematical fine-tuning techniques such as regularisation or parameter isolation. Inspired by the superior adaptability and plasticity of mammalian brains, we posit that neuromodulation with neurotransmitters constitutes a third axis of learning, complementary to neural activity and synaptic plasticity, and should be explicitly modelled in artificial neural networks. In this positional paper, we argue that incorporating neuromodulatory principles into ANN design represents a promising and underexplored research direction, and we advocate for greater attention to this perspective in the development of adaptive and continual learning systems.

</details>
